This table maps out Device Support for Microchip devices.
The value in the MPLAB X column indicates the minimum recommended version of MPLAB X for that
specific device.
The value in the Compiler column indicates the compiler: XC8, XC16, XC-DSC or XC32.
The value in the C Version column indicates the minimum recommended version of the compiler.
>>> A description for each Tool abbreviation can be found at the end of the table. <<<
